=3D"true"><h2>Documentation Index</h2><p>Fetch the complete documentation i=
ndex at: <a href=3D"https://docs.replit.com/llms.txt">https://docs.replit.c=
om/llms.txt</a></p><p>Use this file to discover all available pages before =
exploring further.</p></blockquote><span data-as=3D"p">Autoscale Deployment=
s run on cloud computing resources that scale up and down to efficiently ha=
ndle the
network traffic and workload of your Replit App. When your app is busy, aut=
oscaling adds servers to
manage the load. When your app is idle, it reduces the number to as low as =
zero to save you money.</span>
<span data-as=3D"p">Autoscale Deployments are ideal for the following use c=
ases:</span>
<ul>
<li>Web applications that handle variable workloads and traffic such as eco=
mmerce sites</li>
<li>APIs and services</li>
</ul>

iv></a></div><span class=3D"cursor-pointer">Features</span></h2>
<span data-as=3D"p">Autoscale Deployment include the following features:</s=
pan>
<ul>
<li><strong>Automatic resource scaling</strong>: Automatically adjusts reso=
urces based on traffic patterns to optimize costs.</li>
<li><strong>Custom domains</strong>: Configure a custom domain or use a <co=
de>&lt;app-name&gt;.replit.app</code> URL to access your app.</li>
<li><strong>Configurable limits</strong>: Set the maximum number of instanc=
es your published app can scale to.</li>
<li><strong>Flexible machine power</strong>: Choose the CPU and RAM configu=
ration that meets your app=E2=80=99s needs.</li>
<li><strong>Monitoring</strong>: View logs and monitor your published app=
=E2=80=99s status.</li>
</ul>

</h2>
<span data-as=3D"p">Select <strong>Edit</strong> to view and set the machin=
e power options. Use the sliders to select the CPU and RAM configuration
for each published app server instance.</span>
<span data-as=3D"p">View the <strong>compute unit</strong> cost for the con=
figuration in the <strong>Total per machine</strong> row.
A compute unit is a measurement of cloud computing resources based on the m=
emory and CPU configuration of the machine.</span>
<span data-as=3D"p">To learn more about calculating the cost based on Compu=
te Units, see <a class=3D"link" href=3D"https://docs.replit.com/billing/abo=
ut-usage-based-billing#2-compute-units">Compute Units</a>.</span>

 number of machines</span></h2>
<span data-as=3D"p">Use the slider to adjust the maximum number of machines=
. This number is the upper limit of server
instances the autoscaling feature can assign when it determines your app is=
 busy.</span>
<span data-as=3D"p">The bottom row shows the equivalent compute units, calc=
ulated by the following formula:</span>
<span data-as=3D"p"><code>Number of machines * compute units per machine</c=
ode></span>

.katex { text-rendering: auto; font: 1.21em / 1.2 KaTeX_Main, "Times New Ro=
man", serif; text-indent: 0px; }

.katex * { border-color: currentcolor; }

.katex .katex-version::after { content: "0.16.0"; }

.katex .katex-mathml { clip: rect(1px, 1px, 1px, 1px); border: 0px; height:=
 1px; overflow: hidden; padding: 0px; position: absolute; width: 1px; }

.katex .base { position: relative; white-space: nowrap; width: min-content;=
 }

.katex .base, .katex .strut { display: inline-block; }

.katex .textbf { font-weight: 700; }

.katex .textit { font-style: italic; }

ht: 700; }

-weight: 700; }

le: italic; }

ayout: fixed; }

ttom; }

}

k; width: 100%; }

e .overline-line, .katex .rule, .katex .underline .underline-line { min-hei=

.katex .delimsizing.mult .delim-size1 > span { font-family: KaTeX_Size1; }

.katex .delimsizing.mult .delim-size4 > span { font-family: KaTeX_Size4; }

.katex .nulldelimiter { display: inline-block; width: 0.12em; }

.katex .delimcenter, .katex .op-symbol { position: relative; }

.katex .op-symbol.small-op { font-family: KaTeX_Size1; }

.katex .op-symbol.large-op { font-family: KaTeX_Size2; }

.katex .accent > .vlist-t, .katex .op-limits > .vlist-t { text-align: cente=
r; }

.katex .accent .accent-body { position: relative; }

.katex .accent .accent-body:not(.accent-full) { width: 0px; }

.katex .overlay { display: block; }

.katex .mtable .vertical-separator { display: inline-block; min-width: 1px;=
 }

.katex .mtable .arraycolsep { display: inline-block; }

.katex .mtable .col-align-c > .vlist-t { text-align: center; }

.katex .mtable .col-align-l > .vlist-t { text-align: left; }

.katex .mtable .col-align-r > .vlist-t { text-align: right; }

.katex .svg-align { text-align: left; }

.katex svg { fill: currentcolor; stroke: currentcolor; fill-rule: nonzero; =
fill-opacity: 1; stroke-width: 1; stroke-linecap: butt; stroke-linejoin: mi=
ter; stroke-miterlimit: 4; stroke-dasharray: none; stroke-dashoffset: 0; st=
roke-opacity: 1; display: block; height: inherit; position: absolute; width=
: 100%; }

.katex svg path { stroke: none; }

.katex img { border-style: none; max-height: none; max-width: none; min-hei=
ght: 0px; min-width: 0px; }

.katex .stretchy { display: block; overflow: hidden; position: relative; wi=
dth: 100%; }

.katex .stretchy::after, .katex .stretchy::before { content: ""; }

.katex .hide-tail { overflow: hidden; position: relative; width: 100%; }

.katex .halfarrow-left { left: 0px; overflow: hidden; position: absolute; w=
idth: 50.2%; }

.katex .halfarrow-right { overflow: hidden; position: absolute; right: 0px;=
 width: 50.2%; }

.katex .brace-left { left: 0px; overflow: hidden; position: absolute; width=
: 25.1%; }

.katex .brace-center { left: 25%; overflow: hidden; position: absolute; wid=
th: 50%; }

.katex .brace-right { overflow: hidden; position: absolute; right: 0px; wid=
th: 25.1%; }

.katex .x-arrow-pad { padding: 0px 0.5em; }

.katex .cd-arrow-pad { padding: 0px 0.55556em 0px 0.27778em; }

.katex .mover, .katex .munder, .katex .x-arrow { text-align: center; }

.katex .boxpad { padding: 0px 0.3em; }

.katex .fbox, .katex .fcolorbox { border: 0.04em solid; box-sizing: border-=
box; }

.katex .cancel-pad { padding: 0px 0.2em; }

.katex .cancel-lap { margin-left: -0.2em; margin-right: -0.2em; }

.katex .sout { border-bottom-style: solid; border-bottom-width: 0.08em; }

.katex .angl { border-right: 0.049em solid; border-top: 0.049em solid; box-=
sizing: border-box; margin-right: 0.03889em; }

.katex .anglpad { padding: 0px 0.03889em; }

.katex .eqn-num::before { content: "(" counter(katexEqnNo) ")"; counter-inc=
rement: katexEqnNo 1; }

.katex .mml-eqn-num::before { content: "(" counter(mmlEqnNo) ")"; counter-i=
ncrement: mmlEqnNo 1; }

.katex .mtr-glue { width: 50%; }

.katex .cd-vert-arrow { display: inline-block; position: relative; }

.katex .cd-label-left { display: inline-block; position: absolute; right: c=
alc(50% + 0.3em); text-align: left; }

.katex .cd-label-right { display: inline-block; left: calc(50% + 0.3em); po=
sition: absolute; text-align: right; }

.katex-display { display: block; margin: 1em 0px; text-align: center; }

.katex-display > .katex { display: block; text-align: center; white-space: =
nowrap; }

.katex-display > .katex > .katex-html { display: block; position: relative;=
 }

.katex-display > .katex > .katex-html > .tag { position: absolute; right: 0=
px; }

.katex-display.leqno > .katex > .katex-html > .tag { left: 0px; right: auto=
; }

.katex-display.fleqn > .katex { padding-left: 2em; text-align: left; }

body { counter-reset: katexEqnNo 0 mmlEqnNo 0; }

.latex-style { font-family: "Latin Modern"; line-height: 1.8; max-width: 80=
ch; margin: 0px auto; padding: 2rem 1.25rem; counter-reset: theorem 0 defin=
ition 0 sidenote-counter 0 lemma 0; color: rgb(27, 24, 24); }

.latex-style .theorem { counter-increment: theorem 1; display: block; margi=
n: 12px 0px; font-style: italic; }

.latex-style .theorem::before { content: "Theorem " counter(theorem) ". "; =
font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; }

.latex-style .theorem p { display: unset; }

.latex-style .lemma { counter-increment: lemma 1; display: block; margin: 1=
2px 0px; font-style: italic; }

.latex-style .lemma::before { content: "Lemma " counter(lemma) ". "; font-w=
eight: 700; font-style: normal; }

.latex-style .lemma p { display: unset; }

 position: relative; }

.latex-style .proof::before { content: "Proof.=E2=80=83" attr(title); font-=
style: italic; }

.latex-style .proof::after { position: absolute; right: -12px; bottom: -2px=
; }

.latex-style .proof p { display: unset; }

 margin: 12px 0px; font-style: normal; }

.latex-style .definition::before { content: "Definition " counter(definitio=
n) ". "; font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; }

.28em; margin-left: -0.48em; margin-right: -0.15em; }

: -0.1667em; margin-right: -0.125em; }
